Objective reduced:
```
def softmax_lse_obj(ureduced, beta, n):
    u = np.empty(n); u[0]=0; u[1:]=ureduced
    # shift for exp
    u_shift = u - np.max(u)
    pp = np.exp(u_shift); pp /= pp.sum(); # sum1
    c = convolve_self(pp)
    cm = c.max()
    sh = beta*(c-cm); sh -= sh.max(); esum=np.sum(np.exp(sh))
    obj = 2*n*(cm + np.log(esum)/beta)
    return obj
```

```
def conv_gradient(p, w, n, fsize):
    p_rev = p[::-1]
    L = fsize
    out = np.real(ifft(fft(w, L) * fft(p_rev, L)))
    # Need out[n-1 : 2n-1]
    return 2.0 * out[n-1:2*n-1]
```

Let's design `run_lbfgs_polish(p_in, budget_s, betas, n_label, rng=None, max_attempts=None)`:
Pseudo:
```
def run_lbfgs_polish(p_in, budget_s, betas, rng=None):
    p = clean_p(p_in)
    n=len(p); fsize=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    obj_best = compute_objective(p)
    p_best = p.copy()
    start=time.monotonic()
    call_est = None
    for idx, beta in enumerate(betas):
        if time.monotonic()-start >= budget_s or budget_s <=0: break
        beta_time = budget_s * weight? maybe equal or given
        if remaining small: break
        # maybe split into attempts
        n_attempts=1 if beta_time<30 else 2
        for attempt in range(n_attempts):
             remaining_beta = beta_time/ (n_attempts-attempt)
             if call_est is None: t0; soft_lse_fg; call_est=...
             x0 = u_from_p(p_best or p?)
             if attempt>0: add noise to x0 ~0.01
             maxiter = int(min(1500, max(20, remaining_beta / (25*call_est))))
             maxls = int(min(15, max(4, remaining_beta/(50*call_est))))
             res = scipy_minimize(soft_lse_fg, x0, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, args=(beta,n,fsize), options={...})
             x = res.x if finite else x0
             p_new = p_from_u(x)
             obj_new = compute_objective(p_new)
             if finite and obj_new<obj_best: update; p=p_new else maybe p remains
             print
    return p_best, obj_best
```

```
def run_natural_gradient_polish(p, budget, beta, max_iter, rng):
  p=clean
  lr=0.05  # in log prob scale? p update via exp(lr*du)
  for iters:
    c, M = ...
    w = lse weights with beta (e.g. 10000)
    h = 2n*conv_grad(p,w)
    du = h - dot(p,h)
    # optional normalize du norm
    if norm(du) small break
    for trial in 5: step=lr; p_new=normalize(exp(logp+step*du)); obj exact; if improved break else lr*=0.5
    if no improvement break
```

5. `def lse_weights_sg(c, beta):` maybe returns lse, weights.
```
cm=c.max()

shifted -= shifted.max()
e=np.exp(shifted)
esum=e.sum()
if esum <=0 or not finite: w=uniform; lse=cm
else: w=e/esum; lse=cm + np.log(esum)/beta
return lse,w,cm
```
6. `def gradient_convolution(p, w, n, fsize):`
```
P = rfft(p[::-1], fsize)
W = rfft(w, fsize)
g = irfft(P*W, fsize)
return 2.0 * g[n-1:2*n-1]
```
Need `rfft` of p[::-1] maybe view; okay.


Detailed code for `fft_convolve_self`:
```
def fft_convolve_self(a, fsize=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0)
    if fsize is None:
        fsize = power2_at_least(2*n - 1)
    A = rfft(a, fsize)
    c = irfft(A * A, fsize)
    # Roundoff may produce tiny imaginary? irfft real. Possibly slight negative at ends due roundoff.
    return c[:2*n - 1]
```
For exact max, tiny negative not issue. But if all positive, max positive. For weights, if c has tiny negative at edges, LSE okay. Could clip c to 0? Convolution of nonnegative should be nonnegative, roundoff may produce -1e-18. Use `c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)`? This may alter max extremely. Could do in LSE: `if (c<0).any(): c=np.maximum(c,0)`. But in gradient, weights unchanged. We'll clip c after conv in `lse_weights`? Better `c = np.where(c > 0, c, 0.0)` in functions that use c for weights and exact max. But clipping could hide negative due underflow? fine. However exact objective should match np.convolve; clipping negligible. Use `c = np.clip(c, 0.0, None)` in exact and LSE.

`gradient_convolution` uses p and w. It may produce tiny negative gradient components; okay.

`power2_at_least`: 
```
def power2_at_least(m):
    m = int(max(1,m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()
```

`objectives`:
```
def exact_obj_p(p, fsize=None):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = p.size
    if n == 0: return np.inf
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, None)
    s = p.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300: return np.inf
    p = p / s
    if fsize is None: fsize=power2_at_least(2*n-1)
    c = fft_convolve_self(p, fsize)
    if c.size: c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    val = 2.0 * n * float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(val): return np.inf
    return val
```
`np.max` on empty if n=0; no.

`clean_probability`:
```
def clean_probability(p):
    p=np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if p.size==0: return np.ones(1)
    p=np.clip(p,0.0,1e300)
    s=p.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <=1e-300:
        return np.full(p.size, 1.0/p.size)
    return p/s
```

`u_from_p_scaled`, `p_from_u_scaled` as above.

`soft_lse_fg`:
```
def soft_lse_fg(x, beta, n, fsize, scale):
    if n <= 1:
       return (1e12, np.zeros(0))
    x = np.asarray(x,dtype=np.float64)
    if x.shape[0] != n-1: 
       # fix? return
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)): return (1e12, np.zeros(n-1))
    u = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    u[0]=0.0
    if scale != 1.0:
       u[1:] = x * scale
    else:
       u[1:] = x
    umax = np.max(u)
    if not np.isfinite(umax): return (1e12, np.zeros(n-1))
    e = np.exp(u - umax)
    es = e.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(es) or es <= 1e-300:
       return (1e12, np.zeros(n-1))
    p = e / es
    # p sum 1
    A = rfft(p, fsize)
    c = irfft(A*A, fsize)[:2*n-1]
    c = np.maximum(c,0.0)
    w, lse_val = lse_weights(c, beta)
    # gradient p unscaled
    W = rfft(w, fsize)
    PRev = rfft(p[::-1], fsize)
    grad_p = 2.0 * irfft(W*PRev, fsize)
    if n-1 < len(grad_p): grad_p = grad_p[:n]
    else: grad_p = grad_p[n-1:2*n-1]?
```
Wait `irfft` returns length fsize. We need slice [n-1:2n-1]. Use:
```
gfull = irfft(W * PRev, fsize)
grad_p = 2.0 * gfull[n-1:2*n-1]
```
Yes.
```
    alpha = 2.0 * n
    obj = alpha * lse_val
    h = alpha * grad_p
    m = float(np.dot(p, h))
    grad_u = p * (h - m)
    if scale != 1.0: grad_u = grad_u * scale
    grad_x = grad_u[1:]
    grad_x = np.nan_to_num(grad_x, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)
    if not np.isfinite(obj): obj=1e12
    return (float(obj), grad_x)
```
Need `lse_weights` return weights and lse. Define:
```
def lse_weights(c, beta):
    if beta <=0: return uniform, max(c)
    cm = float(np.max(c))
    shifted = beta * (c - cm)
    shifted -= shifted.max()
    e = np.exp(shifted)
    esum = e.sum()
    if not finite or esum<=0: w=np.ones(len(c))/len(c); lse=cm
    else: w=e/esum; lse=cm + np.log(esum)/beta
    return w, float(lse)
```
If c all zeros? beta shift zeros, e len, lse=0+log(len)/beta >0 but max 0. LSE overestimates if all c zero impossible (p sum1 gives some positive). Fine.

`gradient convolution` no separate needed.

Potential issue: `rfft(p[::-1], fsize)` each call copies. Could precompute? no.

```
def shape_fg(theta, beta, n, fsize, basis):
    theta=np.asarray(theta)
    if theta.size != basis.shape[0]: return (1e12, np.zeros(theta.size))
    if not finite: ...
    v = basis.dot(theta)
    v -= np.mean(v)
    vmax=np.max(v); if not finite...
    e=np.exp(v-vmax); es=e.sum(); p=e/es
    # compute c, weights, h as in soft
    alpha=2*n
    ... grad_p = ...
    h=alpha*grad_p
    # grad theta
    ph = p*h
    vh = basis.dot(ph)
    vp = basis.dot(p)
    mh = float(np.dot(p,h))
    grad = vh - mh*vp
    grad = np.nan_to_num(...)
    obj=alpha*lse_val
    return obj, grad
```

```
  p=clean_probability(p); n=p.size
  if fsize is None: fsize=power2...
  best=p.copy(); best_obj=exact_obj_p(best,fsize)
  mean=1.0/n
  for factor in [1e-3,1e-4,1e-6,1e-8,1e-12]:
     p2=best.copy()
     small=mean*factor
     p2[p2<small]=0.0
     s=p2.sum(); if s<...: continue
     p2/=s
     obj=exact_obj_p(p2,fsize)
     if obj < best_obj:
         best=p2.copy(); best_obj=obj
  # also try set exact zeros if p<1e-15
  p2=best.copy(); p2[best<1e-15]=0; ...
  return best,best_obj
```

```
def random_smooth_p(n, rng, amp=1.0, lowpass_frac=0.02):
    rng = rng
    z = rng.normal(0.0,1.0,n)
    F = rfft(z, n)
    lenF = len(F)
    cutoff = max(2, int((lenF-1) * max(0.0, lowpass_frac)))
    if cutoff >= lenF: cutoff = lenF-1
    F[cutoff:] = 0.0
    v = irfft(F, n)
    v = v - v.mean()
    if amp == 0: p=uniform
    else: vmax=np.max(v); e=np.exp(amp*(v-vmax)); p=e/e.sum()
    return clean_probability(p)
```
If amp negative? use abs. For lowpass_frac 0.02, keep 2% frequencies. Good.

```
def interpolate_sequence(p, n, endpoint=True):
   m=len(p)
   if m==n: q=p.copy()
   else:
      if endpoint: xs=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,m); xl=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
      else: xs=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,m,endpoint=False); xl=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n,endpoint=False)
      xs = (m-1)*np.linspace? np does.
      q = np.interp(xl,xs,p, left=p[0], right=p[-1])
   return clean_probability(q)
```

```
def scaled_betas(n, base=(100,500,2000,10000,40000), cap=200000):
```

Define:
```
def betas_from_objective(obj, n, lambdas=(0.05,0.2,1.0,4.0,16.0), cap=500000.0):
    if not finite or n<=0 or obj<=0: return [100,1000,10000,100000,400000]
    cm = obj/(2.0*n)
    if cm <=1e-300: cm=1.0/ (2.0*n)
    res=[]
    for lam in lambdas:
       b = int(max(1.0, min(cap, lam/cm)))
       res.append(b)
    return res
```
For obj ~1.5,n8192 cm=9.16e-5: betas 546,2184,8736,34944,139775. Good. For n16384 cm=4.58e-5: double. Cap 500k. This is good. Use in all.

```
def coarse_small_search(m, budget_s, rng, p_seed=None, lambdas=(0.1,1,8), cap=500000):

   local_best = clean_probability(p_seed if not None else uniform)

   starts=[]
   if p_seed is not None: starts.append(downsample_p(p_seed,m))
   starts.append(random_smooth_p(m,rng,1.0,0.05))
   starts.append(random_smooth_p(m,rng,2.0,0.02))
   # remove? no
   num=len(starts)
   per=budget/num
   for st in starts:
      if time remaining within budget? We need start time.
      p_new,obj_new=run_full_lbfgs(st, per, rng, lambdas, cap)
      p_new,obj_new=zeroize_probability(p_new)
      if obj_new<obj: update
   return local_best,obj
```

```python
"""
Minimize 2*n*max(convolve(a,a))/(sum(a)**2) over non-negative sequences.

Algorithm:
- Work with probability vectors p (sum p = 1), since the objective is scale invariant.
- Use fast real FFT for full linear convolution and gradients.
- Polish with bounded L-BFGS in a log-probability parametrization
  p = softmax(u), with a stable reference coordinate fixed.
- Explore alternative basins with a low-dimensional cosine/DCT shape search,
  random smooth restarts, coarse-to-fine restarts, and optional larger lengths.
- Finish with exact-peak zeroization and verified final candidates.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft
from scipy.optimize import minimize as scipy_minimize

LOG_BOUND = 40.0
U_SCALE = 10.0
BETA_CAP = 250000.0


def power2_at_least(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def uniform_p(n):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)


def clean_probability(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if p.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1.0e300)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1.0e-300:
        return np.full(p.size, 1.0 / float(p.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return p / s


def fft_convolve_self(a, fsize=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if fsize is None:
        fsize = power2_at_least(2 * n - 1)
    A = rfft(a, fsize)
    c = irfft(A * A, fsize)
    return c[:2 * n - 1]


def exact_obj_p(p, fsize=None):
    p = clean_probability(p)
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    if fsize is None:
        fsize = power2_at_least(2 * n - 1)
    c = fft_convolve_self(p, fsize)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    val = 2.0 * float(n) * float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(val):
        return np.inf
    return val


def lse_weights(c, beta):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.ones(0, dtype=np.float64), 0.0
    cm = float(np.max(c))
    if beta <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(beta):
        w = np.ones(c.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(c.size)
        return w, cm
    shifted = beta * (c - cm)
    shifted = shifted - shifted.max()
    e = np.exp(shifted)
    esum = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(esum) or esum <= 1.0e-300:
        w = np.ones(c.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(c.size)
        lse = cm
    else:
        w = e / esum
        lse = cm + float(np.log(esum)) / beta
    return w, float(lse)


def u_from_p_scaled(p, scale, fix_idx):
    p = clean_probability(p)
    n = p.size
    if n <= 1:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    lp = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1.0e-300))
    lp = lp - lp[fix_idx]
    lp = np.clip(lp, -LOG_BOUND, LOG_BOUND)
    mask = np.ones(n, dtype=bool)
    mask[fix_idx] = False
    out = lp[mask]
    if scale != 1.0:
        out = out / scale
    return out


def p_from_u_scaled(x, scale, fix_idx):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = x.size + 1
    if n <= 1:
        return uniform_p(1)
    if x.size != n - 1:
        x = np.resize(x, n - 1)
    mask = np.ones(n, dtype=bool)
    mask[fix_idx] = False
    u = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    u[mask] = x * scale
    u[fix_idx] = 0.0
    u = np.clip(u, -LOG_BOUND, LOG_BOUND)
    u = u - np.max(u)
    e = np.exp(u)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1.0e-300:
        return uniform_p(n)
    return e / s


def soft_lse_fg(x, beta, n, fsize, scale, fix_idx):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 1:
        return (1.0e12, np.zeros(max(0, n - 1), dtype=np.float64))

    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if x.size != n - 1:
        x = np.resize(x, n - 1)

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return (1.0e12, np.zeros(n - 1, dtype=np.float64))

    mask = np.ones(n, dtype=bool)
    mask[fix_idx] = False

    u = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    u[mask] = x * scale
    u[fix_idx] = 0.0
    u = np.clip(u, -LOG_BOUND, LOG_BOUND)

    u = u - np.max(u)
    e = np.exp(u)
    es = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(es) or es <= 1.0e-300:
        return (1.0e12, np.zeros(n - 1, dtype=np.float64))

    p = e / es

    A = rfft(p, fsize)
    c = irfft(A * A, fsize)[:2 * n - 1]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)

    w, lse_val = lse_weights(c, beta)

    W = rfft(w, fsize)
    PRev = rfft(p[::-1], fsize)
    gfull = irfft(W * PRev, fsize)
    grad_p = 2.0 * gfull[n - 1:2 * n - 1]

    alpha = 2.0 * float(n)
    h = alpha * grad_p
    hsum = float(np.dot(p, h))
    grad_u = p * (h - hsum)
    grad_x = grad_u[mask] * scale

    obj = alpha * float(lse_val)
    if not np.isfinite(obj):
        obj = 1.0e12
    grad_x = np.nan_to_num(grad_x, nan=0.0, posinf=1.0e6, neginf=-1.0e6)
    return (float(obj), grad_x)


def clip_for_output(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return [float(x) for x in p]


def zeroize_probability(p, fsize=None):
    p = clean_probability(p)
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return p, np.inf
    if fsize is None:
        fsize = power2_at_least(2 * n - 1)

    best = p.copy()
    best_obj = exact_obj_p(best, fsize)
    mean = 1.0 / float(n)

    for factor in (1.0e-2, 1.0e-3, 1.0e-4, 1.0e-6, 1.0e-9, 1.0e-12):
        small = mean * factor
        q = best.copy()
        q[q < small] = 0.0
        s = float(q.sum())
        if s <= 1.0e-300:
            continue
        q = q / s
        obj = exact_obj_p(q, fsize)
        if np.isfinite(obj) and obj < best_obj - 1.0e-15:
            best = q.copy()
            best_obj = obj

    q = best.copy()
    q[q < 1.0e-15] = 0.0
    s = float(q.sum())
    if s > 1.0e-300:
        q = q / s
        obj = exact_obj_p(q, fsize)
        if np.isfinite(obj) and obj < best_obj - 1.0e-15:
            best = q.copy()
            best_obj = obj

    return best, best_obj


def interpolate_sequence(p, n, endpoint=True):
    p = clean_probability(p)
    m = p.size
    n = int(max(1, n))
    if m == n:
        return p.copy()
    if m == 1:
        return uniform_p(n)

    if endpoint:
        xs = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
        xl = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    else:
        xs = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m, endpoint=False)
        xl = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=False)

    q = np.interp(xl, xs, p, left=float(p[0]), right=float(p[-1]))
    return clean_probability(q)


def upsample_p(p, n):
    return interpolate_sequence(p, n, endpoint=True)


def downsample_p(p, n):
    return interpolate_sequence(p, n, endpoint=True)


def upsample_variants(p, n):
    a = interpolate_sequence(p, n, endpoint=True)
    b = interpolate_sequence(p, n, endpoint=False)
    return [a, b]


def random_smooth_p(n, rng, amp=1.0, lowpass_frac=0.02):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    z = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)
    F = rfft(z, n)
    lf = len(F)
    cutoff = max(2, int(round((lf - 1) * max(0.0, lowpass_frac))))
    if cutoff >= lf:
        cutoff = lf - 1
    F[cutoff:] = 0.0
    v = irFFT = irfft(F, n)
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    v = v - float(v.mean())

    if abs(float(amp)) < 1.0e-14:
        return uniform_p(n)

    vmax = float(np.max(v))
    e = np.exp(float(amp) * (v - vmax))
    e = np.clip(e, 0.0, 1.0e300)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1.0e-300:
        return uniform_p(n)
    return clean_probability(e / s)


def fit_theta_to_p(p, basis):
    p = clean_probability(p)
    n = p.size
    m = basis.shape[0]
    zeros = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 0 or m == 0:
        return zeros
    lp = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1.0e-300))
    lp = lp - float(np.mean(lp))
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(lp)):
        return zeros
    try:
        coeff = np.linalg.lstsq(basis, lp, rcond=None)[0]
    except Exception:
        return zeros
    coeff = np.nan_to_num(coeff, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return np.clip(coeff, -20.0, 20.0)


def shape_p_from_theta(theta, basis):
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if theta.size != basis.shape[0]:
        theta = np.resize(theta, basis.shape[0])
    v = basis.dot(theta)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(v)):
        return clean_probability(uniform_p(basis.shape[1]))
    v = v - float(np.mean(v))
    v = v - float(np.max(v))
    e = np.exp(v)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1.0e-300:
        return clean_probability(uniform_p(basis.shape[1]))
    return clean_probability(e / s)


def shape_fg(theta, beta, n, fsize, basis):
    n = int(n)
    m = basis.shape[0]
    zeros = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)

    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if theta.size != m:
        theta = np.resize(theta, m)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(theta)):
        return (1.0e12, zeros.copy())

    p = shape_p_from_theta(theta, basis)
    if p.size != n:
        return (1.0e12, zeros.copy())

    A = rfft(p, fsize)
    c = irfft(A * A, fsize)[:2 * n - 1]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)

    w, lse_val = lse_weights(c, beta)

    W = rfft(w, fsize)
    PRev = rfft(p[::-1], fsize)
    gfull = irfft(W * PRev, fsize)
    grad_p = 2.0 * gfull[n - 1:2 * n - 1]

    alpha = 2.0 * float(n)
    h = alpha * grad_p
    ph = p * h
    vh = basis.dot(ph)
    vp = basis.dot(p)
    hsum = float(np.dot(p, h))
    grad = vh - hsum * vp

    obj = alpha * float(lse_val)
    if not np.isfinite(obj):
        obj = 1.0e12
    grad = np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=1.0e6, neginf=-1.0e6)
    return (float(obj), grad)


def betas_from_objective(obj, n, lambdas=(0.05, 0.2, 1.0, 4.0, 16.0), cap=BETA_CAP):
    if n <= 0 or not np.isfinite(obj) or obj <= 0.0:
        return [100, 1000, 5000, 25000, 100000]

    cm = float(obj) / (2.0 * float(n))
    if not np.isfinite(cm) or cm <= 1.0e-300:
        cm = 1.0 / (2.0 * float(n))

    out = []
    for lam in lambdas:
        b = float(lam) / cm
        b = max(10.0, min(float(cap), b))
        out.append(int(b))
    if len(out) == 0:
        out = [1000]
    return out


def run_full_lbfgs(p_in, budget_s, rng, lambdas=(0.05, 0.2, 1.0, 4.0, 16.0),
                   cap=BETA_CAP, u_scale=U_SCALE):
    p = clean_probability(p_in)
    n = p.size
    budget_s = float(max(0.0, budget_s))

    if n <= 1 or budget_s <= 15.0:
        return p, exact_obj_p(p)

    fsize = power2_at_least(2 * n - 1)
    obj_best = exact_obj_p(p, fsize)
    p_best = p.copy()
    start = time.monotonic()

    betas = betas_from_objective(obj_best, n, lambdas, cap)

    fix_idx = int(np.argmax(p_best))
    x_probe = u_from_p_scaled(p_best, u_scale, fix_idx)
    bound = LOG_BOUND / u_scale
    x_probe = np.clip(x_probe, -bound, bound)

    try:
        t0 = time.monotonic()
        soft_lse_fg(x_probe, min(int(betas[0]), 5000), n, fsize, u_scale, fix_idx)
        call_est = max(1.0e-5, time.monotonic() - t0)
    except Exception:
        call_est = 0.005

    bounds = [(-bound, bound)] * max(0, n - 1)
    weights = np.linspace(0.6, 1.7, len(betas), dtype=np.float64)
    weights = weights / weights.sum()

    for bi, beta in enumerate(betas):
        if time.monotonic() - start >= budget_s:
            break
        beta_budget = float(budget_s) * float(weights[bi])
        if beta_budget < 15.0:
            break

        attempts = 2 if beta_budget > 90.0 else 1
        for att in range(attempts):
            if time.monotonic() - start >= budget_s:
                break
            att_budget = beta_budget / float(attempts)
            if att_budget < 10.0:
                break

            fix_idx = int(np.argmax(p_best))
            x0 = u_from_p_scaled(p_best, u_scale, fix_idx)
            if att > 0:
                x0 = x0 + rng.normal(0.0, 0.03, x0.size)
            x0 = np.clip(x0, -bound, bound)

            maxls = int(min(10, max(4, att_budget / max(80.0 * call_est, 1.0e-7))))
            maxiter = int(min(5000, max(50, att_budget / max(float(maxls) * call_est, 1.0e-7))))

            try:
                res = scipy_minimize(
                    soft_lse_fg,
                    x0,
                    method='L-BFGS-B',
                    jac=True,
                    args=(float(beta), int(n), int(fsize), float(u_scale), int(fix_idx)),
                    bounds=bounds,
                    options={
                        'maxiter': maxiter,
                        'maxls': maxls,
                        'ftol': 1.0e-13,
                        'gtol': 1.0e-12,
                        'iprint': -1,
                    }
                )
                xr = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
                if not np.all(np.isfinite(xr)):
                    xr = x0
                xr = np.clip(xr, -bound, bound)
                p_new = p_from_u_scaled(xr, u_scale, fix_idx)
                obj_new = exact_obj_p(p_new, fsize)
                if np.isfinite(obj_new) and obj_new < obj_best - 1.0e-14:
                    p_best = p_new.copy()
                    obj_best = obj_new
                    print("    full-LBFGS n=%d beta=%d attempt=%d obj=%.8f t=%.1f" %
                          (n, int(beta), att + 1, obj_best, time.monotonic() - start))
            except Exception as exc:
                print("    full-LBFGS exception n=%d beta=%d: %s" % (n, int(beta), exc))

    return p_best,(obj_best if np.isfinite(obj_best) else exact_obj_p(p_best, fsize))


def run_shape_search(n, budget_s, rng, start_p=None, M=64,
                     lambdas=(0.1, 1.0, 8.0), cap=BETA_CAP):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    budget_s = float(max(0.0, budget_s))
    if budget_s <= 30.0:
        if start_p is None:
            return uniform_p(n), np.inf
        p0 = clean_probability(start_p)
        return p0, exact_obj_p(p0)

    M = int(max(8, M))
    M = min(M, max(8, n // 16))

    xg = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=True)
    basis = np.empty((M, n), dtype=np.float64)
    for m in range(1, M + 1):
        basis[m - 1] = np.cos(np.pi * float(m) * xg)

    fsize = power2_at_least(2 * n - 1)

    if start_p is not None:
        p_best = clean_probability(start_p)
    else:
        p_best = uniform_p(n)
    obj_best = exact_obj_p(p_best, fsize)
    start_phase = time.monotonic()

    theta_probe = np.zeros(M, dtype=np.float64)
    try:
        t0 = time.monotonic()
        shape_fg(theta_probe, 1000.0, n, fsize, basis)
        call_est = max(1.0e-5, time.monotonic() - t0)
    except Exception:
        call_est = 0.005

    betas = betas_from_objective(obj_best, n, lambdas, cap)

    starts = []
    if start_p is not None:
        starts.append(fit_theta_to_p(p_best, basis))
    starts.append(np.zeros(M, dtype=np.float64))
    for amp in (0.25, 0.6, 1.3, 2.5, 4.5):
        starts.append(rng.normal(0.0, float(amp), M))

    max_starts = int(max(3, min(6, int(budget_s / 60.0) + 1)))
    starts = starts[:max_starts]

    bounds_shape = [(-20.0, 20.0)] * M
    per_beta = budget_s / float(max(1, len(starts) * max(1, len(betas))))
    maxls = int(min(8, max(3, per_beta / max(40.0 * call_est, 1.0e-7))))
    maxiter = int(min(2500, max(30, per_beta / max(float(maxls) * call_est, 1.0e-7))))

    si = 0
    for theta0 in starts:
        if time.monotonic() - start_phase >= budget_s:
            break
        si += 1
        theta0 = np.asarray(theta0, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
        if theta0.size != M:
            theta0 = np.zeros(M, dtype=np.float64)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(theta0)):
            theta0 = np.zeros(M, dtype=np.float64)
        theta0 = np.clip(theta0, -20.0, 20.0)

        for beta in betas:
            if time.monotonic() - start_phase >= budget_s:
                break
            try:
                res = scipy_minimize(
                    shape_fg,
                    theta0,
                    method='L-BFGS-B',
                    jac=True,
                    args=(float(beta), int(n), int(fsize), basis),
                    bounds=bounds_shape,
                    options={
                        'maxiter': maxiter,
                        'maxls': maxls,
                        'ftol': 1.0e-12,
                        'gtol': 1.0e-11,
                        'iprint': -1,
                    }
                )
                th = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
                if th.size != M or not np.all(np.isfinite(th)):
                    th = theta0
                th = np.clip(th, -20.0, 20.0)
                p_new = shape_p_from_theta(th, basis)
                obj_new = exact_obj_p(p_new, fsize)
                if np.isfinite(obj_new) and obj_new < obj_best - 1.0e-14:
                    p_best = p_new.copy()
                    obj_best = obj_new
                    theta0 = th.copy()
                    print("    shape-start=%d beta=%d obj=%.8f t=%.1f" %
                          (si, int(beta), obj_best, time.monotonic() - start_phase))
            except Exception as exc:
                print("    shape exception start=%d beta=%d: %s" % (si, int(beta), exc))

    return p_best, obj_best


def coarse_small_search(m, budget_s, rng, p_seed=None,
                        lambdas=(0.1, 1.0, 8.0), cap=BETA_CAP):
    m = int(max(8, m))
    budget_s = float(max(0.0, budget_s))
    if budget_s <= 10.0:
        if p_seed is None:
            return uniform_p(m), np.inf
        p0 = downsample_p(p_seed, m)
        return p0, exact_obj_p(p0)

    if p_seed is not None:
        local_best = downsample_p(p_seed, m)
    else:
        local_best = uniform_p(m)
    local_obj = exact_obj_p(local_best)

    starts = []
    if p_seed is not None:
        starts.append(downsample_p(p_seed, m))
    starts.append(random_smooth_p(m, rng, 1.0, 0.05))
    starts.append(random_smooth_p(m, rng, 2.0, 0.02))
    starts.append(random_smooth_p(m, rng, 4.0, 0.01))

    per = budget_s / float(max(1, len(starts)))
    start_phase = time.monotonic()

    for p0 in starts:
        if time.monotonic() - start_phase >= budget_s:
            break
        p_try, obj_try = run_full_lbfgs(p0, per, rng, lambdas, cap)
        p_try, obj_try = zeroize_probability(p_try)
        if np.isfinite(obj_try) and obj_try < local_obj - 1.0e-14:
            local_best = p_try.copy()
            local_obj = obj_try
            print("    coarse-small m=%d obj=%.8f" % (m, local_obj))

    return local_best, local_obj


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
    try:
        budget_s = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget_s = 2400.0
    budget_s = max(120.0, budget_s)

    opt_time = min(2340.0, budget_s - 60.0)
    if opt_time < 60.0:
        opt_time = max(30.0, budget_s - 30.0)

    t_start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t_start + max(60.0, opt_time)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Initial sequence
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    a0 = None
    try:
        a0 = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    except Exception:
        a0 = None

    if a0 is not None and a0.size > 8:
        p0 = clean_probability(a0)
        n0 = p0.size
        if 1024 <= n0 <= 16384:
            global_n = int(n0)
            global_p = p0
        elif n0 < 1024:
            global_n = 8192
            global_p = upsample_p(p0, 8192)
        else:
            global_n = 8192
            global_p = downsample_p(p0, 8192)
    else:
        global_n = 8192
        global_p = uniform_p(global_n)

    global_obj = exact_obj_p(global_p)
    if not np.isfinite(global_obj):
        global_n = 8192
        global_p = uniform_p(global_n)
        global_obj = exact_obj_p(global_p)

    print("Initial n=%d obj=%.8f" % (global_n, global_obj))

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Cheap random smooth attempts
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    for amp in (0.25, 0.6, 1.2, 2.5):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 30.0:
            break
        pr = random_smooth_p(global_n, rng, amp, 0.01)
        orbj = exact_obj_p(pr, power2_at_least(2 * global_n - 1))
        if np.isfinite(orbj) and orbj < global_obj - 1.0e-12:
            global_p = pr
            global_obj = orbj
            print("Random smooth amp=%.2f improved obj=%.8f" % (amp, global_obj))

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Low-dimensional cosine shape search
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    shape_p = global_p.copy()
    shape_obj = global_obj
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 120.0:
        M = 48 if global_n > 12000 else 64
        sb = min(300.0, max(60.0, 0.12 * (deadline - time.monotonic())))
        print("\n=== Shape search n=%d budget=%.0fs M=%d ===" % (global_n, sb, M))
        t0 = time.monotonic()
        shape_p, shape_obj = run_shape_search(
            global_n, sb, rng, start_p=global_p, M=M,
            lambdas=(0.1, 1.0, 8.0)
        )
        print("Shape search obj=%.8f t=%.1fs" % (shape_obj, time.monotonic() - t0))
        if np.isfinite(shape_obj) and shape_obj < global_obj - 1.0e-12:
            global_p = shape_p
            global_obj = shape_obj
            print("Shape search improved global obj=%.8f" % global_obj)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Main full-dimensional polishing
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 180.0:
        mb = min(1200.0, max(60.0, 0.45 * (deadline - time.monotonic())))
        print("\n=== Main full L-BFGS n=%d budget=%.0fs ===" % (global_n, mb))
        t0 = time.monotonic()
        global_p, global_obj = run_full_lbfgs(
            global_p, mb, rng,
            lambdas=(0.05, 0.2, 1.0, 4.0, 16.0)
        )
        global_p, global_obj = zeroize_probability(global_p)
        print("Main L-BFGS obj=%.8f t=%.1fs" % (global_obj, time.monotonic() - t0))

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # If the shape search found a near-basin but not global, polish it.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    if (np.isfinite(shape_obj) and shape_obj < global_obj + 0.02 and
            time.monotonic() < deadline - 180.0):
        spb = min(250.0, max(30.0, 0.12 * (deadline - time.monotonic())))
        print("\n=== Polishing shape candidate budget=%.0fs ===" % spb)
        t0 = time.monotonic()
        p_try, obj_try = run_full_lbfgs(
            shape_p, spb, rng,
            lambdas=(0.1, 1.0, 8.0)
        )
        p_try, obj_try = zeroize_probability(p_try)
        print("Shape polish obj=%.8f t=%.1fs" % (obj_try, time.monotonic() - t0))
        if np.isfinite(obj_try) and obj_try < global_obj - 1.0e-12:
            global_p = p_try
            global_obj = obj_try
            print("Shape polish improved global obj=%.8f" % global_obj)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Coarse-to-fine restarts
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    if global_n >= 1024 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 240.0:
        cb = min(250.0, max(60.0, 0.10 * (deadline - time.monotonic())))
        print("\n=== Coarse-to-fine restarts budget=%.0fs ===" % cb)
        t0 = time.monotonic()
        small_lengths = [256, 512]
        per_small = cb / float(len(small_lengths))

        for m in small_lengths:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 80.0:
                break
            try:
                p_small, obj_small = coarse_small_search(
                    m, per_small, rng, p_seed=global_p,
                    lambdas=(0.1, 1.0, 8.0)
                )
                p_up = upsample_p(p_small, global_n)
                obj_up = exact_obj_p(p_up, power2_at_least(2 * global_n - 1))
                print("    coarse m=%d small=%.8f upsampled=%.8f" %
                      (m, obj_small, obj_up))

                if np.isfinite(obj_up) and obj_up < global_obj - 1.0e-12:
                    global_p = p_up
                    global_obj = obj_up
                    print("    coarse-to-fine improved global obj=%.8f" % global_obj)

                if (np.isfinite(obj_up) and obj_up < global_obj + 0.02 and
                        time.monotonic() < deadline - 120.0):
                    b2 = min(90.0, max(20.0, 0.04 * (deadline - time.monotonic())))
                    p_try, obj_try = run_full_lbfgs(
                        p_up, b2, rng,
                        lambdas=(0.1, 1.0, 8.0)
                    )
                    p_try, obj_try = zeroize_probability(p_try)
                    if np.isfinite(obj_try) and obj_try < global_obj - 1.0e-12:
                        global_p = p_try
                        global_obj = obj_try
                        print("    coarse-to-fine polish improved global obj=%.8f" % global_obj)
            except Exception as exc:
                print("    coarse m=%d exception: %s" % (m, exc))

        print("Coarse-to-fine done t=%.1fs" % (time.monotonic() - t0))

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Try larger lengths if there is time.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    for _ in range(2):
        if global_n >= 16384 or time.monotonic() > deadline - 320.0:
            break
        target_n = min(16384, global_n * 2)
        if target_n <= global_n:
            break

        large_budget = min(500.0, max(120.0, 0.30 * (deadline - time.monotonic())))
        print("\n=== Larger length trial n=%d budget=%.0fs ===" % (target_n, large_budget))
        t0 = time.monotonic()

        variants = upsample_variants(global_p, target_n)
        best_q, best_qobj = variants[0], np.inf
        fsize_q = power2_at_least(2 * target_n - 1)
        for q in variants:
            qobj = exact_obj_p(q, fsize_q)
            if np.isfinite(qobj) and qobj < best_qobj:
                best_q, best_qobj = q, qobj

        print("    upsample variants best obj=%.8f" % best_qobj)

        if np.isfinite(best_qobj) and best_qobj < global_obj + 0.05:
            p_try, obj_try = run_full_lbfgs(
                best_q, large_budget, rng,
                lambdas=(0.05, 0.2, 1.0, 4.0)
            )
            p_try, obj_try = zeroize_probability(p_try)
            print("    larger polish obj=%.8f t=%.1fs" % (obj_try, time.monotonic() - t0))
            if np.isfinite(obj_try) and obj_try < global_obj - 1.0e-12:
                global_n = int(target_n)
                global_p = p_try
                global_obj = obj_try
                print("    larger length improved global obj=%.8f n=%d" % (global_obj, global_n))
        else:
            print("    skip larger optimization")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Final high-beta squeeze.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 70.0:
        fb = min(300.0, max(40.0, 0.5 * (deadline - time.monotonic())))
        print("\n=== Final squeeze budget=%.0fs ===" % fb)
        t0 = time.monotonic()
        global_p, global_obj = run_full_lbfgs(
            global_p, fb, rng,
            lambdas=(1.0, 8.0, 32.0)
        )
        global_p, global_obj = zeroize_probability(global_p)
        print("Final squeeze obj=%.8f t=%.1fs" % (global_obj, time.monotonic() - t0))

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Final clean/verification and return.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    pre_p = global_p.copy()
    final_p, final_fft_obj = zeroize_probability(global_p)
    if not np.isfinite(final_fft_obj):
        final_p = pre_p
        final_fft_obj = exact_obj_p(final_p)

    candidates = []
    # Add final zeroized, pre-zeroized, and if possible original input if finite.
    candidates.append(final_p)
    candidates.append(pre_p)
    try:
        if a0 is not None and a0.size == global_n:
            candidates.append(clean_probability(a0))
    except Exception:
        pass

    best_list = None
    best_val = np.inf
    can_verify = (time.monotonic() - t_start) < float(opt_time) + 35.0

    for idx, p_cand in enumerate(candidates):
        if p_cand is None:
            continue
        p_cand = clean_probability(p_cand)
        lst = clip_for_output(p_cand)
        if len(lst) == 0:
            continue
        if not all(np.isfinite(v) for v in lst):
            continue

        val = np.inf
        if can_verify:
            try:
                val = evaluate_sequence(lst)
            except Exception:
                val = np.inf

        if np.isfinite(val):
            if val < best_val:
                best_val = val
                best_list = lst
                print("Candidate %d verified obj=%.10f len=%d" % (idx, val, len(lst)))
        else:
            # Fallback internal evaluation if evaluator cannot be used in time.
            ival = exact_obj_p(p_cand)
            if np.isfinite(ival) and ival < best_val:
                best_val = ival
                best_list = lst
                print("Candidate %d internal obj=%.10f len=%d" % (idx, ival, len(lst)))

    if best_list is None:
        fb_n = 8192
        if 2 <= global_n <= 16384:
            fb_n = int(global_n)
        best_list = clip_for_output(uniform_p(fb_n))
        best_val = np.inf
        try:
            best_val = evaluate_sequence(best_list)
        except Exception:
            pass
        print("Fallback uniform n=%d verified=%s" % (len(best_list), best_val))

    print("Returning best known objective: %s" % (best_val if np.isfinite(best_val) else "inf"))
    return best_list
```
